###############################################################################
# Way of Life: the seduction focus, and lovers
# Namespace: seduction (was WoL.40 - WoL.595 in wol_seduction_events.txt,
#   WoL.1000 - WoL.1164 in wol_lover_events.txt, now seduction.600 - 764, and
#   BRseduction.0 - 11 in BR_WoL_seduction_events.txt, now seduction.800 - 812)
# Faction: Generic
#
# The Seduce decision starts a chase (seduction.100 - 104, with warnings of
# scandal for incest, priests, age and same-sex love). The chase rolls
# hidden outcomes (seduction.110 - 120): love letters, gifts and gossip to
# win the target over, then a dance, a late meeting in the kitchen or a
# picnic in the wild to tempt them into bed. A tumble may bring disease,
# pregnancy, a courtier who saw too much, blackmail or a public scandal.
# Each success may make the seducer better at it, up to the Seducer or
# Seductress trait (seduction.96 - 99).
# The seduction focus also brings chances of its own: a priest at
# confession, a guard or a maid, a lunatic's rose bush or centaur, a gaoler
# who opens the cell and a consort of the harem.
# Lovers (any character, not only the focus): a spouse who suspects an
# affair, lovers who fall out of love, the strain of many lovers, the
# comfort of a lover when the marriage is sour; and monstrous lovers,
# vampires and servants of Chaos who turn on a mortal player who took them
# to bed.
#
# Seduce: start and warnings           seduction.100 - seduction.104
# Seduce: core, scandal, level up      seduction.40 - seduction.120
# Seduce: love letter, gift, gossip    seduction.200 - seduction.223
# Seduce: dance, kitchen, picnic       seduction.300 - seduction.431
# The seduction focus                  seduction.500 - seduction.595
# The lovers' pulse                    seduction.650
# A spouse suspects                    seduction.600 - seduction.641
# Lovers fall out of love              seduction.700 - seduction.709
# Too many lovers                      seduction.750
# The comfort of a lover               seduction.760 - seduction.764
# Monstrous lovers                     seduction.800 - seduction.812
#
# Fired from: the seduce, stop_seduce and break-up decisions in
#   way_of_life_decisions.txt, on_focus_pulse (seduction.500 - 590) and
#   on_yearly_pulse (seduction.650) in 00_on_actions.txt, on_yearly_pulse in
#   wh_on_actions.txt (seduction.800) and the intrigue events (603).
#   Shared effects are in WHG_focus_effects.txt.

###############################################################################

namespace = seduction

###############################################################################
# Seduce: start and warnings
###############################################################################

# seduction.100 Seduce Decision event start - Normal
character_event = {
	id = seduction.100
	title = EVTTITLEseduction100
	desc = EVTDESCseduction100
	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = target_seduced }
		save_event_target_as = target_seducer
		set_character_flag = seduction_confirmed
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction100
		character_event = {
			id = seduction.110
			days = 20
			random = 20
			tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction110
		}
		#		log = "seduction.100: [Root.GetTitledName] begins seduction of [From.GetTitledName]"
	}
}

# seduction.101 Seduce Decision event start - Age scandal risk
character_event = {
	id = seduction.101
	title = EVTTITLEseduction100
	desc = EVTDESCseduction101
	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = target_seduced }
		save_event_target_as = target_seducer
		set_character_flag = seduction_confirmed
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction101
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		trigger = { NOT = { is_older_than = FROM } }
		character_event = {
			id = seduction.110
			days = 20
			random = 20
			tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction110
		}
		#		log = "seduction.101: [Root.GetTitledName] begins seduction of [From.GetTitledName]"
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction101
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		trigger = { is_older_than = FROM }
		character_event = {
			id = seduction.110
			days = 20
			random = 20
			tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction110
		}
		#		log = "seduction.101: [Root.GetTitledName] begins seduction of [From.GetTitledName]"
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseduction101
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seducing } }
	}
}

# Seduce: the core of the chase, scandal and level up
###############################################################################

# seduction.40 Lover's disease notification
character_event = {
	id = seduction.40
	picture = GFX_evt_bad_news
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_disease_immune_race = no
		NOR = { has_character_modifier = minor_disease_herpes trait = lovers_pox }
	}

	desc = { text = EVTDESCseduction40 trigger = { religion_group = old_world_group } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCseduction40_nc trigger = { NOT = { religion_group = old_world_group } } }

	option = {
		name = CURSES
		add_trait = lovers_pox
		nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
	}
}

# seduction.50 Target: Scandal check after Tumble
character_event = {
	id = seduction.50

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = no
				has_regent = no
				in_command = no
				is_adult = yes
				NOT = { trait = blinded }
				NOT = { character = FROM }
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOR = {
					any_spouse = { OR = { character = FROM character = ROOT } }
					any_consort = {
						NOT = { is_senior_consort_party = yes }
						OR = { character = FROM character = ROOT }
					}
				}
				OR = {
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					is_close_relative = FROM
					is_liege_of = ROOT
					is_rival = FROM
					is_rival = ROOT
					is_lover = FROM
					is_lover = ROOT
					has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_chasing_friend }
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						attribute_diff = { attribute = intrigue character = ROOT value = 0 }
						attribute_diff = { attribute = intrigue character = FROM value = 0 }
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_chasing_friend }
					has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_chasing_child }
					has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_chasing_spouse }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							attribute_diff = { attribute = intrigue character = ROOT value = 3 }
							attribute_diff = { attribute = intrigue character = FROM value = 3 }
						}
						has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_chasing_friend }
						has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_chasing_child }
						has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_chasing_spouse }
					}
				}
				random = { chance = 50 character_event = { id = seduction.55 days = 4 random = 7 } }
			}
			else = {
				random = { chance = 25 character_event = { id = seduction.55 days = 4 random = 7 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# seduction.55 Courtier discovers the affair
character_event = {
	id = seduction.55
	title = EVTTITLEseduction55

	picture = GFX_evt_scandal
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROMFROM = { save_event_target_as = target_lover_1 }

		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_spouse = { character = FROM }
					any_consort = { NOT = { is_senior_consort_party = yes } character = FROM }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_female = no }
				opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_unfaithful_wife }
				if = {
					limit = { FROMFROM = { is_female = no } }
					opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_cuckolded }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { gender_equality_trigger = no }
					opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_unfaithful_husband }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { gender_equality_trigger = yes }
					opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_unfaithful_husband_crime }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { FROMFROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
					add_rival = FROMFROM
					opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = slut_seductress }
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_spouse = { character = FROMFROM }
					any_consort = { NOT = { is_senior_consort_party = yes } character = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_female = no }
				opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_unfaithful_wife }
				if = {
					limit = { FROM = { is_female = no } }
					opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_cuckolded }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { gender_equality_trigger = no }
					opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_unfaithful_husband }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { gender_equality_trigger = yes }
					opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_unfaithful_husband_crime }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
					add_rival = FROM
					opinion = { who = FROM modifier = slut_seductress }
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					is_close_relative = event_target:target_lover_1
					NOT = { religion = kitabid }
					NOT = { religion = ormazd }
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = scandal_temp_incest
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROM = { is_female = no same_sex = event_target:target_lover_1 } }
			set_character_flag = scandal_temp_m_homo
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes same_sex = event_target:target_lover_1 } }
			set_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_homo
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					religion_group = old_world_group
					OR = { is_priest = yes is_ascetic_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = scandal_temp_priest
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROM = { age_diff = { who = event_target:target_lover_1 years = 25 } } }
			set_character_flag = scandal_temp_age
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes OR = { is_married = yes is_consort = yes } } }
			set_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_married
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROM = {
					is_woman_trigger = yes
					OR = { is_married = yes NOT = { is_senior_consort_party = yes } }
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = scandal_temp_ff_married
		}
		else = { set_character_flag = scandal_temp_adultery }
	}

	desc = { text = EVTDESCseduction55_cr trigger = { has_character_flag = scandal_temp_incest } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction55_m_homo
		trigger = { has_character_flag = scandal_temp_m_homo }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction55_f_homo
		trigger = { has_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_homo }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction55_priest
		trigger = { has_character_flag = scandal_temp_priest }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction55_age_young
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = scandal_temp_age
			FROM = { NOT = { is_older_than = ROOT_FROMFROM } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction55_age_old
		trigger = { has_character_flag = scandal_temp_age FROM = { is_older_than = ROOT_FROMFROM } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction55_f_married
		trigger = { has_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_married }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction55_ff_married
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = scandal_temp_ff_married
			NOT = { has_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_married }
		}
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCseduction55 trigger = { has_character_flag = scandal_temp_adultery } }

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction55 # Expose them!
		show_portrait = FROMFROM
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = scandal_temp_incest
					has_character_flag = scandal_temp_m_homo
					has_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_homo
					has_character_flag = scandal_temp_priest
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }

		if = { limit = { is_lover = FROM } remove_lover = FROM }
		if = { limit = { is_lover = FROMFROM } remove_lover = FROMFROM }

		FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.60 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction60 } }

		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_age
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_married
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_ff_married
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_adultery
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction55_unholy # Expose the unholy sinners!
		show_portrait = FROMFROM
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = scandal_temp_incest
				has_character_flag = scandal_temp_m_homo
				has_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_homo
				has_character_flag = scandal_temp_priest
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }

		if = { limit = { is_lover = FROM } remove_lover = FROM }
		if = { limit = { is_lover = FROMFROM } remove_lover = FROMFROM }

		FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.60 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction60 } }

		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_incest
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_m_homo
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_homo
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_priest
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction55 # let it pass
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { is_rival = FROM is_rival = FROMFROM } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					is_consort = FROM
					is_consort = FROMFROM
					any_spouse = { OR = { character = FROM character = FROMFROM } }
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = zealous }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = kind }
		}

		if = { limit = { is_lover = FROM } remove_lover = FROM }
		if = { limit = { is_lover = FROMFROM } remove_lover = FROMFROM }

		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_incest
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_m_homo
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_homo
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_priest
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_age
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_married
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_ff_married
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_adultery
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseduction55 # Blackmail the seducer
		show_portrait = FROMFROM
		trigger = {
			NOT = { is_father = FROM }
			NOT = { is_mother = FROM }
			NOT = { is_consort = FROM }
			NOT = { is_consort = FROMFROM }
			NOT = { any_spouse = { OR = { character = FROM character = FROMFROM } } }
			OR = {
				FROMFROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				FROMFROM = { is_married = yes }
				has_character_flag = scandal_temp_incest
				has_character_flag = scandal_temp_m_homo
				has_character_flag = scandal_temp_priest
				has_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_married
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 10.0 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = zealous }
		}

		if = { limit = { is_lover = FROM } remove_lover = FROM }
		if = { limit = { is_lover = FROMFROM } remove_lover = FROMFROM }

		FROMFROM = { letter_event = { id = seduction.56 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction56 } }
	}
}

# seduction.56 The seducer is blackmailed
letter_event = {
	id = seduction.56
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction56
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_character_flag = scandal_temp_incest
						has_character_flag = scandal_temp_m_homo
						has_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_homo
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction56_cr
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = scandal_temp_incest } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction56_homo
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = scandal_temp_m_homo
					has_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_homo
				}
			}
		}
	}

	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction56 # Pay
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		tooltip = { transfer_scaled_wealth = { to = FROM value = 0.2 } }
		FROM = { letter_event = { id = seduction.57 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction57 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction56 # Refuse!
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.58 days = 3 } } }
	}
}

# seduction.57 Blackmailer: The seducer pays
letter_event = {
	id = seduction.57
	desc = EVTDESCseduction57

	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_incest
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_m_homo
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_homo
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_priest
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_age
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_married
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_ff_married
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_adultery
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction57
		FROM = { transfer_scaled_wealth = { to = ROOT value = 0.2 } }
	}
}

# seduction.58 Blackmailer: The seducer refuses to pay. Ping by the target first.
character_event = {
	id = seduction.58

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROMFROM = { letter_event = { id = seduction.59 } }
	}
}

# seduction.59 Blackmailer: The seducer refuses to pay
letter_event = {
	id = seduction.59
	desc = EVTDESCseduction59

	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction59 # Expose them!
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }

		FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.60 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction60 } }

		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_incest
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_m_homo
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_homo
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_priest
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_age
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_f_married
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_ff_married
		clr_character_flag = scandal_temp_adultery
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction59 # Keep quiet anyway

# seduction.60 Seducer: the scandal breaks!
character_event = {
	id = seduction.60
	title = EVTTITLEseduction60
	desc = EVTDESCseduction60

	picture = GFX_evt_scandal
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_adulterer
		FROMFROM = {
			character_event = { id = 20 } # Adultery fallout
			character_event = { id = seduction.61 } # Notification to my lover
		}

		any_spouse = {
			limit = { NOT = { character = FROMFROM } NOT = { character = FROM } }
			character_event = { id = seduction.62 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = { text = EVTOPTAseduction60_f trigger = { FROMFROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAseduction60_m trigger = { FROMFROM = { is_woman_trigger = no } } }

		if = {
			limit = { same_sex = FROMFROM }
			if = {
				limit = { is_female = no }
				tooltip = { add_character_modifier = { name = known_sodomite years = 20 } }
				prestige = -500
				piety = -250
			}
			if = { limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes } prestige = -100 piety = -50 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { age_diff = { who = FROMFROM years = 25 } is_older_than = FROMFROM }
			tooltip = { add_character_modifier = { name = cradle_robber years = 5 } }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				religion_group = old_world_group
				OR = { AND = { is_ruler = yes is_theocracy = yes } is_ascetic_trigger = yes }
			}
			tooltip = { add_character_modifier = { name = adulterous_priest years = 20 } }
			piety = -100
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				is_close_relative = FROMFROM
				NOT = { religion = kitabid }
				NOT = { religion = ormazd }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				tooltip = { add_character_modifier = { name = incestuous_adulteress years = 20 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_female = no }
				tooltip = { add_character_modifier = { name = incestuous_adulterer years = 20 } }
			}

			piety = -100
			prestige = -50
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = no
					is_married = yes
					NOT = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group }
				}
				tooltip = { add_character_modifier = { name = adulterer years = 20 } }
			}

			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				tooltip = { add_character_modifier = { name = adulteress years = 20 } }
				if = { limit = { is_married = no } piety = -50 }
				if = { limit = { is_married = yes } piety = -100 }
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = no
					NOT = { same_sex = FROMFROM }
					FROMFROM = { is_playable = yes }
				}
				if = { limit = { FROMFROM = { tier = COUNT } } prestige = 25 }
				if = { limit = { FROMFROM = { tier = DUKE } } prestige = 50 }
				if = { limit = { FROMFROM = { tier = KING } } prestige = 100 }
				if = { limit = { FROMFROM = { tier = EMPEROR } } prestige = 200 }
			}
		}
	}
}

	desc = EVTDESCseduction61

	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		any_spouse = {
			limit = { NOT = { character = FROMFROM } NOT = { character = FROM } }
			character_event = { id = seduction.63 }
		}
		any_consort = {
			limit = {
				is_senior_consort_party = yes
				NOT = { character = FROMFROM }
				NOT = { character = FROM }
			}
			character_event = { id = seduction.63 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = { text = EVTOPTAseduction61_f trigger = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAseduction60_m trigger = { is_woman_trigger = no } }

		if = {
			limit = { age_diff = { who = FROM years = 25 } is_older_than = FROM }
			tooltip = { add_character_modifier = { name = cradle_robber years = 5 } }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				religion_group = old_world_group
				OR = { is_priest = yes is_ascetic_trigger = yes }
			}
			tooltip = { add_character_modifier = { name = adulterous_priest years = 20 } }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { same_sex = FROM is_female = no }
			tooltip = { add_character_modifier = { name = known_sodomite years = 20 } }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { is_close_relative = FROM }
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				tooltip = { add_character_modifier = { name = incestuous_adulteress years = 20 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_female = no }
				tooltip = { add_character_modifier = { name = incestuous_adulterer years = 20 } }
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_female = no is_married = yes }
				tooltip = { add_character_modifier = { name = adulterer years = 20 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				tooltip = { add_character_modifier = { name = adulteress years = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# seduction.80 Chance of Intrigue boost

	id = seduction.80

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

			limit = { trait = amateurish_plotter }
			random = {
				chance = 10

				character_event = { id = seduction.81 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { trait = flamboyant_schemer }
			random = {
				chance = 5
				set_character_flag = int_ed_boost
				character_event = { id = seduction.81 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { trait = intricate_webweaver }
			random = {
				chance = 1
				set_character_flag = int_ed_boost
				character_event = { id = seduction.81 }
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = wol_court_vixen }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = wol_court_tomcat }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 5
				set_character_flag = int_fox_boost
				character_event = { id = seduction.81 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# seduction.98 Tumble impregnation chance event
character_event = {
	id = seduction.98

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		# STD transmission
		hidden_tooltip = { random = { chance = 50 venereal_disease_effect = yes } }

		# AI affair pregnancy limiter
		if = {
			limit = { ai = yes FROM = { ai = yes } has_character_modifier = lustful_affair_timer }
		}
		else = {
			add_character_modifier = { name = lustful_affair_timer duration = 2190 hidden = yes }

			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_female = no
						age = 45
						is_pregnant = yes
						FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
						NOT = { fertility = 0.2 }
						NOT = { FROM = { fertility = 0.2 } }
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = { limit = { OR = { is_pregnant = yes FROM = { is_pregnant = yes } } } }
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					fertility = 0.6
					OR = { ai = no NOT = { num_of_children = 4 } }
					FROM = { fertility = 0.6 OR = { ai = no NOT = { num_of_children = 4 } } }
				}
				random = {
					chance = 50
					if = { limit = { is_married = no is_consort = no } impregnate = FROM }
					else = { impregnate_cuckoo = FROM }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					fertility = 0.5
					OR = { ai = no NOT = { num_of_children = 6 } }
					FROM = { fertility = 0.5 OR = { ai = no NOT = { num_of_children = 6 } } }
				}
				random = {
					chance = 33
					if = { limit = { is_married = no is_consort = no } impregnate = FROM }
					else = { impregnate_cuckoo = FROM }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { fertility = 0.34 FROM = { fertility = 0.34 } }
				random = {
					chance = 15
					if = { limit = { is_married = no is_consort = no } impregnate = FROM }
					else = { impregnate_cuckoo = FROM }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random = {
					chance = 5
					if = { limit = { is_married = no is_consort = no } impregnate = FROM }
					else = { impregnate_cuckoo = FROM }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# seduction.96 Seducer / Seductress level up chance
character_event = {
	id = seduction.96

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		# Chance of trait adjustments
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { trait = chaste }
				NOT = { trait = lustful }
				random = 85 # 15% chance
			}
			add_trait = lustful
			character_event = { id = 38272 } # Lustful notification
		}

		if = {
			limit = { trait = chaste random = 50 }
			remove_trait = chaste
			character_event = { id = 38303 } # Chaste loss notification
		}

		# Chance of various Intrigue boosters
		character_event = { id = seduction.80 days = 7 }

		# Chance of levelling up Seducer skills
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2 }
			random = {
				chance = 10
				character_event = { id = seduction.99 days = 4 } # Gain the Master Seducer trait
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1 }
			random = {
				chance = 20
				remove_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1
				add_character_modifier = { modifier = wol_seducer_2 duration = -1 }
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { NOR = { trait = seducer trait = seductress } }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_character_modifier = { modifier = wol_seducer_1 duration = -1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# seduction.110 Hidden 'outcome tombola' event
character_event = {
	id = seduction.110
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROM = { has_character_modifier = dismissed_proposal } # My target is known to refuse seduction
			}
			character_event = { id = seduction.119 }
			character_event = { id = seduction.118 }
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = yes # I have become imprisoned
			}
			character_event = { id = seduction.118 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				trait = incapable # I have become incapable
			}
			character_event = { id = seduction.118 }
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_focus = focus_seduction } # I have changed my focus
			}
			character_event = { id = seduction.118 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_seducing } }
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { FROMFROM = { OR = { is_married = ROOT is_consort = ROOT } } }
			character_event = { id = seduction.114 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROMFROM = { is_alive = no } }
			character_event = { id = seduction.111 }
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { FROMFROM = { prisoner = yes } }
			character_event = { id = seduction.112 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROMFROM = { trait = incapable } }
			character_event = { id = seduction.113 }
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_regent = yes
					in_command = yes
					FROMFROM = {
						OR = {
							trait = on_pilgrimage
							trait = in_hiding
							in_command = yes

					}

				chance = 80

						location = { distance = { who = FROMFROM value = 400 } }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 2.0
						location = { distance = { who = FROMFROM value = 500 } }
					}
					repeat_event = { id = seduction.110 days = 20 random = 20 } # Fire again with no scope changes

					random = {
						chance = 66
						character_event = { id = seduction.115 } # Time to give up?
					}
				}
				100 = { }
			}
		}

					}

						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_chance_meeting } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.350 } } # Chance Meeting
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_picnic } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.400 } } # Picnic
					}
					else = {
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.300 } } # Dance
					}
				}

					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_chance_meeting } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.350 } } # Chance Meeting
					}

						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_picnic } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.400 } } # Picnic
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_dance } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.300 } } # Dance
					}

		}
		else_if = {
			# The target does not like me much
			limit = { FROMFROM = { personal_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 } } }

			random_list = {
				# Seduction attempts
				13 = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_dance } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.300 } } # Dance
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_chance_meeting } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.350 } } # Chance Meeting
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_picnic } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.400 } } # Picnic
					}
					else = {
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.300 } } # Dance
					}
				}
				13 = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_chance_meeting } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.350 } } # Chance Meeting
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_picnic } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.400 } } # Picnic
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_dance } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.300 } } # Dance
					}
					else = {
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.350 } } # Chance Meeting
					}
				}
				13 = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_picnic } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.400 } } # Picnic
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_dance } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.300 } } # Dance
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_chance_meeting } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.350 } } # Chance Meeting
					}
					else = {
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.400 } } # Picnic
					}
				}
				# Gifts/wooing
				21 = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_love_letter } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.200 } } # Love letter
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_gift } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.210 } } # Necklace / Sword
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_gossip } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.220 } } # Gossiping Maid/Squire
					}
					else = {
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.200 } } # Love letter
					}
				}
				20 = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_gift } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.210 } } # Necklace / Sword
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_gossip } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.220 } } # Gossiping Maid/Squire
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_love_letter } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.200 } } # Love letter
					}
					else = {
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.210 } } # Necklace / Sword
					}
				}
				20 = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_gossip } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.220 } } # Gossiping Maid/Squire
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_love_letter } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.200 } } # Love letter
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_gift } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.210 } } # Necklace / Sword
					}
					else = {
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.220 } } # Gossiping Maid/Squire
					}
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			# The target dislikes me
			limit = { FROMFROM = { NOT = { personal_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 } } } }

			random_list = {
				# Gifts/wooing
				34 = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_love_letter } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.200 } } # Love letter
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_gift } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.210 } } # Necklace / Sword
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_gossip } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.220 } } # Gossiping Maid/Squire
					}
					else = {
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.200 } } # Love letter
					}
				}
				33 = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_gift } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.210 } } # Necklace / Sword
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_gossip } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.220 } } # Gossiping Maid/Squire
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_love_letter } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.200 } } # Love letter
					}
					else = {
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.210 } } # Necklace / Sword
					}
				}
				33 = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_gossip } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.220 } } # Gossiping Maid/Squire
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_love_letter } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.200 } } # Love letter
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = wol_seduce_gift } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.210 } } # Necklace / Sword
					}
					else = {
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.220 } } # Gossiping Maid/Squire
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# seduction.115 This is taking too long. Give up?
character_event = {
	id = seduction.115

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction115_busy
		trigger = { OR = { has_regent = yes in_command = yes } }
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction115_unavailable
		trigger = {
			FROMFROMFROM = {
				OR = {
					trait = on_pilgrimage
					trait = in_hiding
					in_command = yes
					has_character_modifier = in_meditation
					has_character_modifier = doing_penance
					AND = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion NOT = { at_location = ROOT } }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction115
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_regent = yes
				in_command = yes
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = {
						trait = on_pilgrimage
						trait = in_hiding
						in_command = yes
						has_character_modifier = in_meditation
						has_character_modifier = doing_penance
						AND = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion NOT = { at_location = ROOT } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	show_from_from_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction115 # Keep trying
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FROMFROMFROM = { has_character_modifier = lustful_affair_timer }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction115 # Give up
		ai_chance = { factor = 5 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seduction.118 } }
	}
}

# seduction.118 End of Seduction event to clear out flags, etc
character_event = {
	id = seduction.118
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		remove_opinion = { who = event_target:target_seduced modifier = opinion_seducing }

		clear_delayed_event = { id = seduction.110 }

		clr_character_flag = wol_seduce_love_letter
		clr_character_flag = wol_seduce_gift
		clr_character_flag = wol_seduce_gossip
		clr_character_flag = wol_seduce_dance
		clr_character_flag = wol_seduce_chance_meeting
		clr_character_flag = wol_seduce_picnic
		clr_character_flag = seduction_confirmed
	}
}

# seduction.200 Opinion booster - Love Letter
character_event = {
	id = seduction.200
	title = EVTTITLEseduction200

	picture = GFX_evt_gift
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction200
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { is_close_relative = FROM }
				has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted }
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction200_cr
		trigger = {
			is_close_relative = FROM
			NOT = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted } }
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { set_character_flag = wol_seduce_love_letter }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction200 # How romantic!
		trigger = { NOT = { is_close_relative = FROM } }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = chaste }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_fair_trait_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { trait = leper } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { trait = imbecile } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { trait = inbred } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = harelip } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = hunchback } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = dwarf } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = cynical }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = arbitrary }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = trusting }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2.25 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 5.0 FROM = { OR = { trait = seducer trait = seductress } } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = harelip }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = hunchback }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = dwarf }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = inbred }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = leper }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = lustful }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_repelled } }
			}
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_repelled } }
			remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_repelled }
		}

		FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.201 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction201 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction200 # How delightfully perverted!
		trigger = { is_close_relative = FROM }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = chaste }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_fair_trait_trigger = yes }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				NOT = { OR = { trait = arbitrary trait = lustful trait = hedonist } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { trait = leper } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { trait = imbecile } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { trait = inbred } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = harelip } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = hunchback } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = dwarf } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = cynical }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = trusting }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2.25 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 5.0 FROM = { OR = { trait = seducer trait = seductress } } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = harelip }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = hunchback }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = dwarf }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = inbred }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = leper }
		}

			limit = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted } }

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTFseduction200 # This is sick!
		trigger = { is_close_relative = FROM }

				factor = 2.0
				OR = { trait = cynical trait = paranoid }

					trait = hunchback
				}

							has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes
							trait = leper
							trait = dwarf
							trait = harelip
							trait = hunchback
							trait = inbred
							trait = imbecile

					NOT = { num_of_dynasty_members = 2 }
					AND = {

						mother = { lower_tier_than = FROM }
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				OR = {
					trait = lustful
					trait = hedonist
					has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1

					trait = seducer

				factor = 2.0
				OR = {
					higher_tier_than = FROM
					father = { higher_tier_than = FROM }
					mother = { higher_tier_than = FROM }
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = chaste }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				is_married = yes
				spouse = { reverse_personal_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }

				is_married = yes
				spouse = { reverse_personal_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 75 } }

# seduction.201 Opinion booster - Love Letter - Positive response

	id = seduction.201
	title = EVTTITLEseduction201
	desc = EVTDESCseduction201

	picture = GFX_evt_gift
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }

			limit = {
				is_female = no
				FROM = {
					is_woman_trigger = yes
					OR = {
						higher_tier_than = duke
						any_spouse = { higher_tier_than = duke }
						any_consort = { higher_tier_than = duke }
					}
				}
			}
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = { id = seduction.110 days = 60 random = 30 } # Back to the tombola
			}
		}
		else = {
			character_event = {
				id = seduction.110
				days = 20
				random = 20
				tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction110
			} # Back to the tombola
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction201 # I have tired of this sport
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }

		# End the seduction
		custom_tooltip = { text = WOL_SEDUCE_END_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seduction.118 } }
	}
}

# seduction.210 Opinion booster - Gift (necklace or sword)
character_event = {
	id = seduction.210
	title = EVTTITLEseduction210

	picture = GFX_evt_gift
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction210
		trigger = {
			is_woman_trigger = yes
			OR = {
				NOT = { is_close_relative = FROM }
				has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted }
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction210_cr
		trigger = {
			is_woman_trigger = yes
			is_close_relative = FROM
			NOT = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted } }
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction210_m
		trigger = {
			is_woman_trigger = no
			OR = {
				NOT = { is_close_relative = FROM }
				has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted }
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction210_cr_m
		trigger = {
			is_woman_trigger = no
			is_close_relative = FROM
			NOT = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted } }
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { set_character_flag = wol_seduce_gift }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction210 # How romantic!
		trigger = { NOT = { is_close_relative = FROM } }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = chaste }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_fair_trait_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { trait = leper } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { trait = imbecile } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { trait = inbred } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = harelip } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = hunchback } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = dwarf } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = cynical }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = arbitrary }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = trusting }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2.25 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 5.0 FROM = { OR = { trait = seducer trait = seductress } } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = harelip }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = hunchback }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = dwarf }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = inbred }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = leper }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = lustful }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_repelled } }
			}
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_repelled } }
			remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_repelled }
		}

		FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.211 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction201 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction210 # How delightfully perverted!
		trigger = { is_close_relative = FROM }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = chaste }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_fair_trait_trigger = yes }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				NOT = { OR = { trait = arbitrary trait = lustful trait = hedonist } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { trait = leper } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { trait = imbecile } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { trait = inbred } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = harelip } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = hunchback } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = dwarf } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = cynical }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = trusting }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2.25 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 5.0 FROM = { OR = { trait = seducer trait = seductress } } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = harelip }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = hunchback }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = dwarf }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = inbred }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = leper }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {

			}

			remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_repelled }
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 100 modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = cruel } }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.212 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction202 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDseduction200 # I am married
		trigger = { is_married = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = cruel }
			modifier = {

				NOT = {
					OR = { trait = lustful trait = deceitful trait = arbitrary trait = hedonist }
				}
				spouse = { reverse_personal_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 25 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				NOT = {
					OR = { trait = lustful trait = deceitful trait = arbitrary trait = hedonist }
				}
				spouse = { reverse_personal_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }
			}

				NOT = {
					OR = { trait = lustful trait = deceitful trait = arbitrary trait = hedonist }
				}
				spouse = { is_lover = ROOT }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.212 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction202 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEseduction200 # I am offended
		trigger = { NOT = { is_close_relative = FROM } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = cruel }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				OR = { trait = cynical trait = paranoid }
				OR = {
					has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes
					trait = leper
					trait = dwarf
					trait = harelip
					trait = hunchback
				}
				FROM = {
					NOT = {
						OR = {
							has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes
							trait = leper
							trait = dwarf
							trait = harelip
							trait = hunchback
							trait = inbred
							trait = imbecile
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		if = {

				NOT = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted } }
			}

		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted } }
			remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted }
		}

		FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.213 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction203 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTFseduction200 # This is sick!
		trigger = { is_close_relative = FROM }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = cruel }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				NOT = { OR = { trait = arbitrary trait = lustful trait = hedonist } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				OR = { trait = cynical trait = paranoid }
				OR = {
					has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes
					trait = leper
					trait = dwarf
					trait = harelip
					trait = hunchback
				}
				FROM = {
					NOT = {
						OR = {
							has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes
							trait = leper
							trait = dwarf
							trait = harelip
							trait = hunchback
							trait = inbred
							trait = imbecile
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted } }
			}
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_repelled }

		if = {
			limit = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted } }

		FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.213 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction203 } }
	}

		name = EVTOPTseduction_refuse

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50

				factor = 0.2
				is_married = no
				is_consort = no

					is_lowborn = yes
					NOT = { num_of_dynasty_members = 2 }
					AND = {
						lower_tier_than = FROM
						father = { lower_tier_than = FROM }
						mother = { lower_tier_than = FROM }
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				OR = {
					trait = lustful
					trait = hedonist
					has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1
					has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2
					trait = seductress
					trait = seducer
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 FROM = { OR = { trait = seductress trait = seducer } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { trait = deceitful trait = arbitrary } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = stubborn }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				OR = {
					higher_tier_than = FROM
					father = { higher_tier_than = FROM }
					mother = { higher_tier_than = FROM }
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = chaste }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				is_married = yes
				spouse = { reverse_personal_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				is_married = yes
				spouse = { reverse_personal_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 75 } }
			}
		}
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_refused months = 60 }
		add_character_modifier = { name = dismissed_proposal years = 5 }

		FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.120 } }
	}
}

# seduction.221 Opinion booster - Gossip - Positive response
character_event = {
	id = seduction.221
	title = EVTTITLEseduction221
	desc = EVTDESCseduction221

	picture = GFX_evt_gift
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction221 # Great!
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_female = no
				FROM = {
					is_woman_trigger = yes
					OR = {
						higher_tier_than = duke
						any_spouse = { higher_tier_than = duke }
						any_consort = { higher_tier_than = duke }
					}
				}
			}
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = { id = seduction.110 days = 60 random = 30 } # Back to the tombola
			}
		}
		else = {
			character_event = {
				id = seduction.110
				days = 20
				random = 20
				tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction110
			} # Back to the tombola
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction221 # I have tired of this sport
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
		# End the seduction
		custom_tooltip = { text = WOL_SEDUCE_END_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seduction.118 } }
	}
}

			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = mystic }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = erudite }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = scholar }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 society_member_of = hermetics }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { society_rank >= 3 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 FROM = { OR = { trait = seducer trait = seductress } } }
		}

		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = seduction.306 days = 1 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction306_2 }
		}
		clr_character_flag = seduction_whats_your_zodiac
	}

	option = { # Spurn
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBseduction352_poet
			trigger = { has_character_flag = seduction_whisper_poet }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBseduction352_lewd
			trigger = { has_character_flag = seduction_whisper_lewd }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBseduction352_love
			trigger = { has_character_flag = seduction_whisper_love }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBseduction352_breath
			trigger = { has_character_flag = seduction_whisper_breath }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBseduction352_astrology_compatible
			trigger = {
				has_character_flag = seduction_whats_your_zodiac
				FROM = { is_romantic_zodiac_match_with_root_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBseduction352_astrology_mismatch
			trigger = {
				has_character_flag = seduction_whats_your_zodiac
				FROM = { is_romantic_zodiac_match_with_root_trigger = no }
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = seduction_whats_your_zodiac
				FROM = { is_romantic_zodiac_match_with_root_trigger = yes }
			}
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBseduction352_astrology_compatible_tt }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = seduction_whats_your_zodiac
				FROM = { is_romantic_zodiac_match_with_root_trigger = no }
			}
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBseduction352_astrology_mismatch_tt }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 10.0
				is_woman_trigger = yes
				NOT = { trait = lustful }
				NOT = { trait = hedonist }
				NOT = { trait = seducer }
				NOT = { trait = seductress }
				NOT = { trait = arbitrary }
				is_dumb_trigger = no
				NOT = { has_focus = focus_seduction }
				OR = {
					trait = craven
					trait = chaste
					any_spouse = {
						OR = {
							is_lover = ROOT
							ROOT = { opinion_diff = { first = PREV second = FROM value = 10 } }
						}
					}
					any_consort = {
						OR = {
							is_lover = ROOT
							ROOT = { opinion_diff = { first = PREV second = FROM value = 10 } }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				set_character_flag = seduction_rebuff_351
				character_event = { id = seduction.353 days = 1 }
			}
		}

		clr_character_flag = seduction_whisper_love
		clr_character_flag = seduction_whisper_lewd
		clr_character_flag = seduction_whisper_poet
		clr_character_flag = seduction_whisper_breath
		clr_character_flag = seduction_whats_your_zodiac
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTseduction_refuse

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { ai = no } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				is_married = no
				is_consort = no
				OR = {
					is_lowborn = yes
					NOT = { num_of_dynasty_members = 2 }
					AND = {
						lower_tier_than = FROM
						father = { lower_tier_than = FROM }
						mother = { lower_tier_than = FROM }
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				OR = {
					trait = lustful
					trait = hedonist
					has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1
					has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2
					trait = seductress
					trait = seducer
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 FROM = { OR = { trait = seductress trait = seducer } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { trait = deceitful trait = arbitrary } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = stubborn }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				OR = {
					higher_tier_than = FROM
					father = { higher_tier_than = FROM }
					mother = { higher_tier_than = FROM }
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = chaste }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				is_married = yes
				spouse = { reverse_personal_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				is_married = yes
				spouse = { reverse_personal_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 75 } }
			}
		}
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_refused months = 60 }
		add_character_modifier = { name = dismissed_proposal years = 5 }

		FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.120 } }
	}
}

			is_woman_trigger = no
		}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction353_old_f
		trigger = {
			NOT = { is_close_relative = FROM }
			age_diff = { who = FROM years = 25 }
			is_older_than = FROM
			is_woman_trigger = yes
		}
	}

		trigger = {

			age_diff = { who = FROM years = 25 }
			NOT = { is_older_than = FROM }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction353
		trigger = {
			NOT = { is_close_relative = FROM }
			NOT = { age_diff = { who = FROM years = 25 } }
		}
	}

	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction307
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }

		clr_character_flag = seduction_rebuff_351
		clr_character_flag = seduction_rebuff_401

		if = {
			limit = {
				is_female = no
				FROM = {
					is_woman_trigger = yes
					OR = {
						higher_tier_than = duke
						any_spouse = { higher_tier_than = duke }
						any_consort = { higher_tier_than = duke }
					}
				}
			}
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = { id = seduction.110 days = 60 random = 30 } # Back to the tombola
			}
		}
		else = {
			character_event = {
				id = seduction.110
				days = 20
				random = 20
				tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction110
			} # Back to the tombola
		}
	}

	option = { # End it, I'm bored
		name = EVTOPTBseduction307
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = lustful FROM = { ai = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 200 FROM = { ai = no } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = WOL_SEDUCE_END_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seduction.118 } }

		clr_character_flag = seduction_rebuff_351
		clr_character_flag = seduction_rebuff_401
	}
}

# seduction.401 Seducer: Attempts to get target alone
character_event = {
	id = seduction.401
	title = EVTTITLEseduction401

	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction401
		trigger = { same_realm = FROM NOT = { is_close_relative = FROM } NOT = { same_sex = FROM } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction401_distant
		trigger = {
			NOT = { same_realm = FROM }
			NOT = { is_close_relative = FROM }
			NOT = { same_sex = FROM }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction401_homo
		trigger = { NOT = { is_close_relative = FROM } same_sex = FROM }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCseduction401_cr trigger = { is_close_relative = FROM } }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Find an ally among target's friends
		name = EVTOPTAseduction401
		trigger = { NOT = { is_close_relative = FROM } NOT = { same_sex = FROM } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				random_courtier = {
					limit = {
						prisoner = no
						ai = yes
						same_sex = PREV
						is_adult = yes
						NOR = {
							character = ROOT
							character = PREV
							trait = incapable
							trait = on_pilgrimage
							trait = in_hiding
							in_command = yes
							has_character_modifier = in_meditation
							has_character_modifier = doing_penance
							AND = {
								has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
								NOT = { at_location = PREV }
							}
							is_father = PREV
							is_mother = PREV
							is_lover = PREV
							is_rival = PREV
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = seduction.402 days = 1 }
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = seduction_ally_found }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = seduction_ally_found } }
				FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.413 days = 1 } } # Failed to find a courtier
			}
			clr_character_flag = seduction_ally_found
		}
	}

	option = { # Leave a note for target
		name = EVTOPTBseduction401
		trigger = { NOT = { is_close_relative = FROM } NOT = { same_sex = FROM } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				random_courtier = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							character = PREV # The target
							AND = {
								prisoner = no
								is_adult = yes
								NOT = { character = ROOT }
								NOT = { character = PREV }
								NOT = { trait = incapable }
								NOR = {
									trait = on_pilgrimage
									trait = in_hiding
									in_command = yes
									has_character_modifier = in_meditation
									has_character_modifier = doing_penance
								}

								at_location = PREV
								OR = {
									AND = {
										OR = {
											has_opinion_modifier = {
												who = ROOT
												modifier = opinion_chasing_friend
											}
											has_opinion_modifier = {
												who = ROOT
												modifier = opinion_chasing_spouse
											}
											has_opinion_modifier = {
												who = ROOT
												modifier = opinion_chasing_child
											}
										}
										random = 50 # 50% chance
									}
									AND = {
										OR = {
											any_spouse = { character = PREVPREV }
											is_father = PREV
											is_liege_of = PREV
										}
										random = 90 # 10% chance
									}
									random = 98 # 2% chance
								}
							}
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = seduction.417 days = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Simply take a walk with target
		name = EVTOPTCseduction401
		trigger = { OR = { same_sex = FROM is_close_relative = FROM } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }

		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seduction.431 days = 1 } }
	}
	option = { # Take what chances I can to build attraction
		name = EVTOPTDseduction401
		trigger = { NOT = { is_close_relative = FROM } NOT = { same_sex = FROM } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.415 days = 1 } } }
	}
}

		}
	}

		trigger = {

			NOT = { has_character_flag = seduction_outing_note }
		}

		name = EVTOPTAseduction352_poet

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50 # Lower base chance
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 NOT = { personal_opinion = { who = FROM value = 20 } } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				is_close_relative = FROM
				NOT = { religion = kitabid }
				NOT = { religion = ormazd }
				NOT = { OR = { trait = arbitrary trait = lustful trait = hedonist } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 personal_opinion = { who = FROM value = 40 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 personal_opinion = { who = FROM value = 60 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 FROM = { OR = { trait = seducer trait = seductress } } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				NOT = { learning = 5 }
				NOT = { trait = scholar }
				NOT = { trait = mystic }
				NOT = { trait = poet }
				NOT = { trait = mastermind_theologian }
				NOT = { trait = scholarly_theologian }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.25
				NOT = { age = 25 }
				NOT = { trait = scholar }
				NOT = { trait = mystic }
				NOT = { trait = poet }
				NOT = { trait = mastermind_theologian }
				NOT = { trait = scholarly_theologian }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 10.0 trait = poet }
			modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = scholar }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = mystic }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 learning = 10 }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 learning = 12 }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 learning = 15 }
			modifier = {
				factor = 4.0

# seduction.405 Seducer: Success End
character_event = {
	id = seduction.405
	title = EVTTITLEseduction401

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction405_sin
		trigger = { OR = { is_close_relative = FROM same_sex = FROM } }
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction405
		trigger = { NOT = { is_close_relative = FROM } NOT = { same_sex = FROM } }
	}

	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		# Chance of levelling up Seduction skill
		character_event = { id = seduction.96 }

		# End the Seduction
		character_event = { id = seduction.118 }

		# Scandal check
		FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.50 } }

		# Renown: the seducer
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = chronicle.95 } }

		# Chance of impregnation
		if = {
			limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
			FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.97 } }
		}
		else = { FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.98 } } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction306 # Love and leave
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { num_of_lovers = 1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 10 num_of_lovers = 1 }
			modifier = { factor = 10 num_of_lovers = 2 }
			modifier = { factor = 10 num_of_lovers = 3 }
			modifier = { factor = 10 num_of_lovers = 4 }
			modifier = { factor = 10 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = arbitrary }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseduction306_custom }
		if = {
			limit = { has_quest = quest_chaos_cults_seduce }
			add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
			clr_quest = quest_chaos_cults_seduce
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction306 # Our love must grow
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = { is_no_females_race = no FROM = { is_no_females_race = no } }
			add_lover = FROM
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_quest = quest_chaos_cults_seduce }
			add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
			clr_quest = quest_chaos_cults_seduce
		}
	}
}

# seduction.407 Seducer: Courtier refuses to assist
character_event = {
	id = seduction.407
	title = EVTTITLEseduction401

	desc = EVTDESCseduction407

	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	show_from_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction407
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }

		if = {
			limit = {
				is_female = no
				FROM = {
					is_woman_trigger = yes
					OR = {
						higher_tier_than = duke
						any_spouse = { higher_tier_than = duke }
						any_consort = { higher_tier_than = duke }
					}
				}
			}
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = { id = seduction.110 days = 60 random = 30 } # Back to the tombola
			}
		}
		else = {
			character_event = {
				id = seduction.110
				days = 20
				random = 20
				tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction110
			} # Back to the tombola
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction407 # I have tired of this sport
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = lustful FROM = { ai = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 200 FROM = { ai = no } }
		}
		# End the seduction
		custom_tooltip = { text = WOL_SEDUCE_END_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seduction.118 } }
	}
}

# seduction.415 Seduced: charmed
character_event = {
	id = seduction.415
	title = EVTTITLEseduction401

	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	desc = { text = EVTDESCseduction415 trigger = { same_realm = FROM } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCseduction415_distant trigger = { NOT = { same_realm = FROM } } }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction415
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_repelled } }
			}
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_repelled } }
			remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_seduced_repelled }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.416 } } }
	}

# seduction.417 Target or courtier: A note found, addressed to the target
character_event = {
	id = seduction.417
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = { limit = { character = FROMFROM } character_event = { id = seduction.418 } }
		else_if = {
			limit = { any_spouse = { character = FROMFROM } }
			character_event = { id = seduction.421 }
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = seduction.424 } # Courtier or parent
		}
	}
}

# seduction.418 Seduced: finds a note
character_event = {
	id = seduction.418
	title = EVTTITLEseduction401

	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	desc = { text = EVTDESCseduction418 trigger = { same_realm = FROMFROM } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCseduction418_distant trigger = { NOT = { same_realm = FROMFROM } } }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_flattered months = 12 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction418_f # Go meet Seducer by the stream
		trigger = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
		ai_chance = { factor = 200 }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.419 } } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction418_m # Go meet Seductress by the stream
		trigger = { is_female = no }
		ai_chance = { factor = 200 }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.419 } } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction418 # Ignore
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_seduced_attracted }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = lustful }
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = hedonist }
		}

		hidden_tooltip = { FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.420 } } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTseduction_refuse

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROMFROM = { ai = no } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				is_married = no
				is_consort = no
				OR = {
					is_lowborn = yes
					NOT = { num_of_dynasty_members = 2 }
					AND = {
						lower_tier_than = FROMFROM
						father = { lower_tier_than = FROMFROM }
						mother = { lower_tier_than = FROMFROM }
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				OR = {
					trait = lustful
					trait = hedonist
					has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1
					has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2
					trait = seductress
					trait = seducer
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 FROMFROM = { OR = { trait = seductress trait = seducer } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 FROMFROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROMFROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { trait = deceitful trait = arbitrary } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = stubborn }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				OR = {
					higher_tier_than = FROMFROM
					father = { higher_tier_than = FROMFROM }
					mother = { higher_tier_than = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = chaste }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				is_married = yes
				spouse = { reverse_personal_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				is_married = yes
				spouse = { reverse_personal_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 75 } }
			}
		}

		opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_seduced_refused months = 60 }
		add_character_modifier = { name = dismissed_proposal years = 5 }

		FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.120 } }
	}
}

			trigger = {

					is_woman_trigger = no
					OR = {

						NOT = { is_older_than = ROOT }
					}
				}
			}
		}

			trigger = {

					is_older_than = ROOT
				}
			}

						NOT = { is_older_than = ROOT }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAseduction409_f_old
			trigger = {
				FROMFROM = {
					is_woman_trigger = yes
					age_diff = { who = ROOT years = 10 }
					is_older_than = ROOT
				}
			}
		}

		trigger = { FROMFROMFROM = { is_child_of = ROOT } }

		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }

		opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_chasing_child months = 36 }

		hidden_tooltip = { FROMFROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.425 } } }
	}

	option = { # Other courtier found the note
		name = EVTOPTBseduction424

		trigger = { FROMFROMFROM = { NOT = { is_child_of = ROOT } } }

		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }

		opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_chasing_friend months = 36 }

		hidden_tooltip = { FROMFROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.425 } } }
	}

	option = { # Amorous courtier goes to the meeting instead
		name = EVTOPTCseduction424

		trigger = {
			FROMFROMFROM = { same_sex = ROOT }
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { same_sex = ROOT }
					AND = { trait = homosexual ROOT = { trait = homosexual } }
				}
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10

			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = shy }
		}

		hidden_tooltip = { FROMFROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.427 } } }
	}
}

			character_event = { id = seduction.96 }

			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.97 } }
			}

			if = {
				limit = { is_female = no }
				FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.98 } }
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				is_female = no

					is_woman_trigger = yes
					OR = {
						higher_tier_than = duke
						any_spouse = { higher_tier_than = duke }
						any_consort = { higher_tier_than = duke }
					}
				}
			}
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = { id = seduction.110 days = 60 random = 30 } # Back to the tombola
			}
		}
		else = {
			character_event = {
				id = seduction.110
				days = 20
				random = 20
				tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction110
			} # Back to the tombola
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction428 # Ehm, sorry, but no!

		FROMFROM = {
			character_event = { id = seduction.430 days = 1 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction430 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				is_female = no
				FROM = {
					is_woman_trigger = yes
					OR = {
						higher_tier_than = duke
