# seduction.500 Flirting at confession - attraction vs priest
character_event = {
	id = seduction.500
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_focus_pulse (was MTTH 60 months)

	desc = EVTDESCseduction500
	picture = GFX_evt_monk
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	min_age = 16
	max_age = 65
	only_playable = yes
	religion_group = old_world_group
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		has_focus = focus_seduction
		is_ill = no
		has_regent = no
		OR = { religion = sigmarite religion = ulrican religion = myrmidian religion = shallyan }
		any_realm_character = {
			is_priest = yes
			religion = ROOT
			ai = yes
			prisoner = no
			has_regent = no
			is_ill = no
			NOT = { age = 65 }
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					trait = incapable
					trait = infirm
					trait = eunuch
					trait = celibate
					trait = inbred
					trait = imbecile
				}
			}
			NOT = { is_lover = ROOT }
			NOT = { character = ROOT }
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = homosexual ROOT = { trait = homosexual } same_sex = ROOT }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = homosexual }
					NOT = { ROOT = { trait = homosexual } }
					NOT = { same_sex = ROOT }
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_seduced_lustful_worship }
			}
			NOT = { is_close_relative = ROOT }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 4
			OR = {
				trait = arbitrary
				trait = lustful
				trait = cynical
				trait = hedonist
				trait = seducer
				trait = seductress
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = chaste }
		modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = zealous }
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.25
			OR = {
				has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes
				trait = dwarf
				trait = inbred
				trait = imbecile
				trait = clubfooted
				trait = harelip
				trait = hunchback
				trait = gluttonous
				trait = eunuch
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = just }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_realm_character = {
			limit = {
				is_priest = yes
				religion = ROOT
				ai = yes
				prisoner = no
				has_regent = no
				is_ill = no
				NOT = { age = 65 }
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						trait = incapable
						trait = infirm
						trait = eunuch
						trait = celibate
						trait = inbred
						trait = imbecile
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_lover = ROOT }
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = homosexual ROOT = { trait = homosexual } same_sex = ROOT }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = homosexual }
						NOT = { ROOT = { trait = homosexual } }
						NOT = { same_sex = ROOT }
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_seduced_lustful_worship }
				}
				NOT = { is_close_relative = ROOT }
			}
			save_event_target_as = target_priest
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction500 # Go for it
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }

		event_target:target_priest = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_seduced_lustful_worship }
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			# Chance of impregnation
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				event_target:target_priest = { character_event = { id = seduction.97 } }
			}
			else = { event_target:target_priest = { character_event = { id = seduction.98 } } }
		}

		#		log = "seduction.500: [Root.GetTitledName] tumbles priest - [target_priest.GetTitledName]"
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction500 # No
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 30 # More dangerous for fertile married women
				is_woman_trigger = yes
				NOT = { age = 45 }
				OR = { is_consort = yes is_married = yes }
				NOT = {
					OR = { trait = homosexual trait = arbitrary trait = lustful trait = hedonist }
				}
			}
		}

		piety = 10
	}
}

# seduction.520 Female: Seduce handsome guard. Can appear at court (chance of Syphilitic, Cruel, Pregnancy).
character_event = {
	id = seduction.520
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_focus_pulse (was MTTH 60 months)

	desc = EVTDESCseduction520
	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	only_playable = yes
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		has_focus = focus_seduction
		is_pregnant = no
		is_ill = no
		has_regent = no
		OR = { is_female = no NOT = { age = 65 } }
		OR = {
			AND = { is_woman_trigger = yes NOT = { trait = homosexual } }
			AND = { is_female = no trait = homosexual }
		}
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = hidden_seduced_bodyguard }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.2 # More dangerous for fertile married women
			is_woman_trigger = yes
			NOT = { age = 45 }
			OR = { is_consort = yes is_married = yes }
			NOT = { OR = { trait = homosexual trait = arbitrary trait = lustful trait = hedonist } }
		}

		modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = chaste }
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.25
			OR = {
				has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes
				trait = dwarf
				trait = inbred
				trait = imbecile
				trait = clubfooted
				trait = harelip
				trait = hunchback
				trait = gluttonous
				trait = eunuch
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = just }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = zealous }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction520 # Tumble and make lover (appears at court)

		ai_chance = { factor = 5 }

		add_character_modifier = { modifier = hidden_seduced_bodyguard years = 30 hidden = yes }

		create_character = {
			dynasty = none
			religion = ROOT
			culture = ROOT
			female = no
			age = 17
			trait = tough_soldier
			trait = strong
			trait = fair
			trait = diligent
			trait = trusting
			trait = brave
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			new_character = {
				# Set Racial Trait
				character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_no_females_race = no ROOT = { is_no_females_race = no } }
					add_lover = ROOT
				}
				if = { limit = { same_sex = ROOT } add_trait = homosexual }
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_seduced_lustful_worship years = 30 }
				random = {
					chance = 25
					character_event = { id = health.19 days = 15 random = 5 } #Gives lover's pox
				}
			}

			# Chance of impregnation
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				new_character = { character_event = { id = seduction.97 } }
			}
		}

		#		log = "seduction.520: [Root.GetTitledName] creates lover courtier [New.GetBestName]"
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction520 # Tumble and make bodyguard (grants a nice character modifier)

		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }

		add_character_modifier = { modifier = seduced_loyal_bodyguard years = 30 }

		add_character_modifier = { modifier = hidden_seduced_bodyguard years = 30 hidden = yes }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes NOT = { age = 45 } }
				random = {
					chance = 33
					if = { limit = { is_married = no is_consort = no } impregnate = yes }
					else = { impregnate_cuckoo = yes }
				}
			}
			random = {
				chance = 25
				character_event = { id = seduction.40 days = 7 } # Chance of VD
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseduction520 # No

		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }

		piety = 5
	}
}

	id = seduction.562
	desc = EVTDESCseduction562
	picture = GFX_evt_birth
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_tooltip = { # Hidden so the head of faith does not appear as a portrait for affected characters
			k_cult_of_sigmar = {
				holder_scope = {
					create_character = {
						dynasty = none
						religion = old_faith
						culture = albion_main
						female = yes
						dna = "gppniiosfag"
						age = 20
					}
					new_character = {
						# Set Racial Trait
						character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
						ROOT = {
							random_list = {
								50 = {
									create_character = {
										dynasty = none
										religion = ROOT
										culture = ROOT

										genetic_mother = PREV
										genetic_father = ROOT
										female = no
										age = 0
										trait = bastard
										trait = fair
									}
									new_character = {
										# Set Racial Trait
										character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
										save_event_target_as = target_baby
									}
								}
								50 = {
									create_character = {
										dynasty = none
										religion = ROOT
										culture = ROOT
										race = PREV
										genetic_mother = PREV
										genetic_father = ROOT
										female = yes
										age = 0
										trait = bastard
										trait = fair
									}
									new_character = {
										# Set Racial Trait
										character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
										save_event_target_as = target_baby
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
						else = { death = { death_reason = death_natural } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction562
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		event_target:target_baby = { set_father = ROOT }

		if = {
			limit = { is_married = yes }
			spouse = {
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_unfaithful_private years = 10 }
				hidden_tooltip = {
					event_target:target_baby = {
						reverse_opinion = { who = PREV modifier = opinion_whorespawn years = 100 }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { trait = zealous }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				remove_trait = zealous
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38310 days = 1 } # Notify zealous lost
				}
			}
		}
		#		log = "seduction.562: [Root.GetTitledName] has rose bush baby [target_baby.GetFirstName] (lunatic)"
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction562
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# seduction.580 Prisoner seduces guard -> sprung from jail
character_event = {
	id = seduction.580
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_focus_pulse (was MTTH 60 months)
	desc = EVTDESCseduction580
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	only_playable = yes
	max_age = 45
	only_women = yes
	prisoner = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		has_focus = focus_seduction
		is_ill = no
		NOT = {
			OR = {
				trait = incapable
				trait = infirm
				trait = eunuch
				trait = celibate
				trait = inbred
				trait = imbecile
			}
		}
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = hidden_seduced_gaoler }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = chaste }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = lustful }
		modifier = { factor = 2 has_fair_trait_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.1 has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = dwarf }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction580 # Try
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = hidden_seduced_gaoler years = 10 hidden = yes }

			if = {
				limit = { OR = { trait = seductress has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2 } }
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						character_event = { id = seduction.581 days = 1 } # Fail
					}
					75 = {
						character_event = { id = seduction.585 days = 1 } # Succeed
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					60 = {
						character_event = { id = seduction.581 days = 1 } # Fail
					}
					40 = {
						character_event = { id = seduction.585 days = 1 } # Succeed
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction580 # No
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 30.0 trait = craven } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = depressed } }
			random = {
				chance = 20
				add_trait = depressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38288 } # Notify Depressed
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = impaler }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 NOT = { opinion = { who = FROM value = -50 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 opinion = { who = FROM value = 50 } }
		}

		tooltip = { FROM = { add_character_modifier = { name = the_oubliette duration = -1 } } }

		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.584 } } }
	}
}

# seduction.590 Seduction of a Concubine
character_event = {
	id = seduction.590
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_focus_pulse (was MTTH 360 months)
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction590
		trigger = { event_target:target_consort = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction590_male
		trigger = { event_target:target_consort = { is_woman_trigger = no } }
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_sultan
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	only_playable = yes
	prisoner = no
	only_capable = yes
	only_men = yes

	trigger = {
		has_focus = focus_seduction
		has_concubinage = yes
		is_ill = no
		NOT = {
			OR = {
				trait = incapable
				trait = infirm
				trait = eunuch
				trait = celibate
				trait = inbred
				trait = imbecile
			}
		}
		any_consort = {
			NOT = { is_senior_consort_party = yes }
			NOT = { is_lover = ROOT }
			prisoner = no
			NOT = { OR = { trait = incapable trait = infirm trait = celibate } }
			NOT = { age = 40 }
		}
		NOT = { has_any_opinion_modifier = opinion_seducing_concubine }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = gregarious }
		modifier = { factor = 2 has_fair_trait_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { trait = seducer has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2 } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.33 trait = kind }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = harelip }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = clubfooted }
		modifier = { factor = 0.33 trait = impaler }
		modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = shy }
		modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = cruel }
		modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = hunchback }
		modifier = { factor = 0.1 has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = dwarf }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_consort = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_lover = ROOT }
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { OR = { trait = incapable trait = infirm trait = celibate } }
				NOT = { age = 40 }
			}
			save_event_target_as = target_consort
			reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_seducing_concubine months = 1200 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction590
		event_target:target_consort = {
			character_event = { id = seduction.591 days = 1 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction591 }
		}
	}
}

# seduction.591 Concubine reacts
character_event = {
	id = seduction.591
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = OK # Opinion boost / become lover
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_forced_consort } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 FROM = { has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 FROM = { trait = dwarf } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.33 FROM = { trait = hunchback } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { trait = cruel } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { trait = shy } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = clubfooted } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = harelip } }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 FROM = { trait = kind } }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 FROM = { has_fair_trait_trigger = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 FROM = { trait = gregarious } }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 5.0 FROM = { trait = seducer } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = content }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = trusting }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.75
				has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_forced_consort }
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_forced_consort } }
			FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.594 days = 7 random = 7 } } # Forgives abduction
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { opinion = { who = FROM value = 50 } }
			FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.593 days = 7 random = 7 } } # Becomes lover
		}
		else = {
			FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.592 days = 7 random = 7 } } # Opinion increase
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = OK # Opinion decreases.
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.595 days = 7 random = 7 } }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Lovers (was wol_lover_events.txt)
###############################################################################

# seduction.650 The lovers' pulse (on_yearly_pulse): once a year a ruler
# whose spouse strays may grow suspicious (600), and a ruler with a lover
# may turn on a lover who cares little for them (700), tire of juggling
# many lovers (750) or meet a married lover's spouse (760). When the ruler
# is the one who strays, an unlanded spouse may grow suspicious instead
# (landed spouses roll on their own pulse). Each chance and its modifiers
# follow the old mean times to happen.
# seduction.650 Scope: a ruler.
character_event = {
	id = seduction.650
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_lover = yes any_spouse = { has_lover = yes } }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_spouse = { has_lover = yes NOT = { is_lover = ROOT } } }
			save_event_target_as = seduction_suspicious_spouse
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_lover = yes any_spouse = { is_ruler = no } }
			random_spouse = {
				limit = { is_ruler = no }
				save_event_target_as = seduction_suspicious_spouse
			}
		}
		event_target:seduction_suspicious_spouse = {
			random = {
				chance = 3
				modifier = { factor = 4 trait = paranoid }
				modifier = { factor = 4 trait = genius }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = schemer }
				modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { trait = quick trait = shrewd } }
				modifier = { factor = 4 has_focus = focus_intrigue }
				modifier = { factor = 1.33 trait = envious }
				modifier = { factor = 1.33 trait = ambitious }
				modifier = { factor = 1.33 trait = just }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = arbitrary }
				modifier = { factor = 0.33 trait = content }
				modifier = { factor = 0.33 OR = { trait = slow trait = dull } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = trusting }
				character_event = { id = seduction.600 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_lover = yes }
			random = {
				chance = 10
				modifier = { factor = 5 trait = cruel }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cynical }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = arbitrary }
				modifier = { factor = 1.33 trait = wroth }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = patient }
				modifier = { factor = 0.33 trait = kind }
				modifier = { factor = 0.33 has_focus = focus_seduction }
				character_event = { id = seduction.700 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { num_of_lovers = 2 }
				random = {
					chance = 14
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = depressed }
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = slothful }
					modifier = { factor = 1.33 num_of_lovers = 3 }
					modifier = { factor = 2 num_of_lovers = 4 }
					modifier = { factor = 2 num_of_lovers = 6 }
					modifier = { factor = 2 num_of_lovers = 8 }
					modifier = { factor = 2 num_of_lovers = 10 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.33 trait = wroth }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = patient }
					modifier = { factor = 0.67 trait = ambitious }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = diligent }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						OR = {
							trait = seducer
							trait = seductress
							has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2
							has_focus = focus_seduction
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = seduction.750 }
				}
			}
			random = {
				chance = 8
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = kind }
				modifier = { factor = 1.33 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = depressed }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = stressed }
				modifier = { factor = 0.33 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 0.33 trait = cruel }
				character_event = { id = seduction.760 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# seduction.600 Hidden start event to set up the scopes
character_event = {
	id = seduction.600
	is_triggered_only = yes # seduction.650, the lovers' pulse (was MTTH 360 months)
	hide_window = yes

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	min_age = 16
	is_married = yes

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Way of Life"
		OR = { has_polygamy = no is_female = no }
		NOT = { trait = infirm }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		NOT = { trait = on_pilgrimage }
		NOT = { trait = imbecile }
		NOT = { trait = inbred }

		in_command = no

		any_spouse = {
			NOT = { culture_group = daemonic_group }
			prisoner = no
			NOT = { trait = infirm }
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			NOT = { trait = on_pilgrimage }
			any_lover = {
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = wol_court_tomcat }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = wol_court_vixen }
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { is_consort = PREV }
			}
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = wol_court_tomcat }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = wol_court_vixen }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		# It is ok for this to be a bit more selective than the 'any_spouse' trigger above, since this event is invisible.
		random_spouse = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { trait = infirm }
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				NOT = { trait = on_pilgrimage }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = wol_court_tomcat }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = wol_court_vixen }
				NOT = { reverse_has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_cuckolded } }
				NOT = {
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = opinion_cuckolded_private
					}
				}
				NOT = { reverse_has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = slut_seductress } }
				NOT = { reverse_has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_duped } }
				any_lover = {
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = wol_court_tomcat }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = wol_court_vixen }
					prisoner = no
					NOT = { is_consort = PREV }
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = seduction.601 }
		}
	}
}

# seduction.603 Spouse suspected of affair - Choices

		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction603_cr
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = tipped_off_about_spouse_affair }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = spy_spouse_affair }
			FROMFROM = { ROOT = { FROM = { is_close_relative = PREVPREV } } }
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction603_tip
		trigger = { has_character_flag = tipped_off_about_spouse_affair }

		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction603 # Confront Spouse
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = honest } }

			clr_character_flag = tipped_off_about_spouse_affair
			clr_character_flag = spy_spouse_affair

		}
	}

	option = {
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBseduction603_f # Confront the Lover
			trigger = { FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBseduction603_m # Confront the Lover

		}

			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = deceitful }

			modifier = { factor = 3.0 is_lover = FROMFROM }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clr_character_flag = tipped_off_about_spouse_affair
			clr_character_flag = spy_spouse_affair

		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseduction603_homo_f # Two women? Who cares...
		trigger = { FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } FROMFROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		clr_character_flag = tipped_off_about_spouse_affair
		clr_character_flag = spy_spouse_affair
	}

	option = {
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTCseduction603 # Focus on more important matters
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					open_follower_of_slaanesh = yes
					religion = cult_of_pleasure
					religion = atharti
				}
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTCseduction603_fun # They are just having some fun
			trigger = {
				OR = {
					open_follower_of_slaanesh = yes
					religion = cult_of_pleasure
					religion = atharti
				}
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				FROM = { is_female = no }
				FROMFROM = { is_female = no }
				open_follower_of_slaanesh = yes
				religion = cult_of_pleasure
				religion = atharti
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = {
				factor = 300.0
				OR = {
					open_follower_of_slaanesh = yes
					religion = atharti
					religion = cult_of_pleasure
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					trait = depressed
					trait = arbitrary
					open_follower_of_slaanesh = yes
					religion = atharti
					religion = cult_of_pleasure
				}
			}
			random = {
				chance = 20
				add_trait = depressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38288 } # Depressed gained
				}
			}
		}

		clr_character_flag = tipped_off_about_spouse_affair
		clr_character_flag = spy_spouse_affair
	}
}

# seduction.604 Spouse is confronted
character_event = {

			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = just }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { personal_opinion = { who = FROM value = 0 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 NOT = { personal_opinion = { who = FROM value = -50 } } }
		}

		FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.605 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction605 } } # Lover notified
		remove_lover = FROMFROM

		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = seduction.606 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction606 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction604 # Cruelly admit
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 0 is_close_relative = FROMFROM }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = arbitrary }
			modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = cruel }
		}

		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = seduction.609 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction609 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseduction604 # Lie and deny
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = deceitful } }

		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = seduction.622 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction622 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDseduction604 # Skillfully manipulate (high intrigue)
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = {
			attribute_diff = { attribute = intrigue character = FROM value = 5 }
			FROM = { NOT = { has_focus = focus_intrigue } }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 10000 }

		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = seduction.610 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction610 }
		}
	}
}

# seduction.606 The spouse responds to the tearful confession
character_event = {
	id = seduction.606
	picture = GFX_evt_quarrel
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	desc = EVTDESCseduction606

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction606 # Forgive and move on
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 personal_opinion = { who = FROM value = 50 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = patient }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 is_lover = FROM }
		}
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_unfaithful_private months = 24 }
		if = {
			limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes FROMFROMFROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
			opinion = { who = FROMFROMFROM modifier = slut_seductress months = 120 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_female = no FROMFROMFROM = { is_female = no } }
			opinion = { who = FROMFROMFROM modifier = opinion_cuckolded_private months = 240 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction606 # Denounce them both (scandal)
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				is_ruler = no
				FROM = { is_ruler = yes }
				NOT = { trait = arbitrary }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 NOT = { personal_opinion = { who = FROM value = 0 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = cruel }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				NOR = {
					open_follower_of_slaanesh = yes
					religion = cult_of_pleasure
					religion = atharti
				}
			}
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_unfaithful_wife months = 120 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				OR = {
					open_follower_of_slaanesh = yes
					religion = cult_of_pleasure
					religion = atharti
				}
			}
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_unfaithful months = 120 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { is_female = no } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						gender_equality_trigger = no
						open_follower_of_slaanesh = yes
						religion = cult_of_pleasure
						religion = atharti
					}
				}
				opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_unfaithful_husband months = 120 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					gender_equality_trigger = no
					NOR = {
						open_follower_of_slaanesh = yes
						religion = cult_of_pleasure
						religion = atharti
					}
				}
				opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_unfaithful_husband_crime months = 120 }
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				is_female = no
				FROMFROMFROM = { is_female = no }
				NOR = {
					religion = cult_of_pleasure
					religion = atharti
					open_follower_of_slaanesh = yes
				}
			}
			opinion = { who = FROMFROMFROM modifier = opinion_cuckolded months = 120 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_female = no
				FROMFROMFROM = { is_female = no }
				OR = {
					religion = cult_of_pleasure
					religion = atharti
					open_follower_of_slaanesh = yes
				}
			}
			opinion = { who = FROMFROMFROM modifier = opinion_duped months = 120 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes FROMFROMFROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
			opinion = { who = FROMFROMFROM modifier = slut_seductress months = 120 }
		}

		if = { limit = { is_lover = FROM } remove_lover = FROM }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROMFROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.607 } }
			FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.608 } }
		}
	}
}

# seduction.622 The spouse denies the affair
character_event = {
	id = seduction.622
	picture = GFX_evt_quarrel
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	desc = EVTDESCseduction622

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction622 # Very well...
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 personal_opinion = { who = FROM value = 50 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = patient }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 is_lover = FROM }
			modifier = { factor = 10.0 trait = trusting }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = trusting } }
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_suspected_adultery months = 24 }

			opinion = { who = FROMFROMFROM modifier = opinion_chasing_spouse months = 24 }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { trait = arbitrary } }
				random = {
					chance = 20
					add_trait = depressed
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = { id = 38288 } # Depressed gained
					}
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = paranoid } NOT = { trait = trusting } }
			random = {
				chance = 10
				add_trait = paranoid
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38275 } # Paranoid gained
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { trait = trusting }
			random = {
				chance = 20
				remove_trait = trusting
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38306 } # Trusting lost
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction622 # The lover is married too... Tip off his/her spouse.
		trigger = { FROMFROMFROM = { is_married = yes } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { trait = slow trait = dull } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = patient }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 2.5 OR = { trait = quick trait = shrewd } }
			modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = genius }
		}
		FROMFROMFROM = {
			character_event = { id = seduction.623 days = 1 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction623 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseduction622 # Pay to have them both watched
		trigger = { wealth = 10 }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { trait = slow trait = dull } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = deceitful }
			modifier = { factor = 10.0 trait = paranoid }
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROMFROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.626 days = 30 random = 30 } }
		}
	}
}

					has_character_modifier = wol_court_vixen

			modifier = {
				factor = 5.0
				FROMFROM = {
					OR = {
						has_character_modifier = wol_court_vixen

						religion = cult_of_pleasure
						gender_equality_trigger = no
					}
				}
				opinion = { who = FROMFROMFROM modifier = opinion_unfaithful_husband months = 120 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					gender_equality_trigger = yes
					NOR = {
						open_follower_of_slaanesh = yes
						religion = atharti
						religion = cult_of_pleasure
					}
				}
				opinion = {
					who = FROMFROMFROM
					modifier = opinion_unfaithful_husband_crime
					months = 120
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				is_female = no
				FROM = { is_female = no }
				NOR = {
					open_follower_of_slaanesh = yes
					religion = atharti
					religion = cult_of_pleasure
				}
			}
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_cuckolded months = 120 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_female = no
				FROM = { is_female = no }
				OR = {
					open_follower_of_slaanesh = yes
					religion = atharti
					religion = cult_of_pleasure
				}
			}
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_duped months = 120 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = slut_seductress months = 120 }
		}

		if = { limit = { is_lover = FROMFROMFROM } remove_lover = FROMFROMFROM }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROMFROMFROM = {
				character_event = { id = seduction.629 }
				character_event = { id = seduction.631 }
			}
		}
	}
}

					character_event = { id = 38308 } # Scarred gained
				}
			}
			34 = { }

			FROM = { set_character_flag = wol_lover_assaults }

character_event = {

					hidden_tooltip = {

					}

			}
		}
	}

	}

			trigger = { has_character_flag = wol_lover_assaults }
		}

			factor = 100

			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = patient }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = kind }

			modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = trusting }

					}
				}
			}
		}

				chance = 10
				add_trait = paranoid
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38275 } # Paranoid gained
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { trait = trusting }
			random = {
				chance = 20
				remove_trait = trusting
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38306 } # Trusting lost
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseduction622 # Pay to have them both watched
		trigger = { wealth = 10 }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { trait = slow trait = dull } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = deceitful }
			modifier = { factor = 10.0 trait = paranoid }

		}

			FROMFROM = { NOT = { has_focus = focus_intrigue } }

	}

				factor = 3.0
				NOT = { combat_rating_diff = { character = FROM value = -30 } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 5.0 FROM = { trait = duelist } }
		}

		remove_lover = FROMFROM

		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.619 days = 2 } } # Notify break-up
		}
	}
}

# seduction.635 Accuser: Duel outcome
character_event = {
	id = seduction.635
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = combatant_1 #Required for the evaluation below...
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = combatant_2 }
		evaluate_duel_result_score_effect = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = OK # Accuser wins.
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = duelist }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = berserker }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 duelist_skill_is_high_group_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 duel_result_score_high_group_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 combat_rating_diff = { character = FROM value = 10 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 combat_rating_diff = { character = FROM value = 30 } }
		}

		character_event = { id = seduction.636 } # Notify
	}

	option = {
		name = OK # Defender wins
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 FROM = { trait = duelist } }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 FROM = { trait = berserker } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { duelist_skill_is_high_group_trigger = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 duel_result_score_low_group_trigger = yes }
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				FROM = { combat_rating_diff = { character = ROOT value = 10 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				FROM = { combat_rating_diff = { character = ROOT value = 30 } }
			}
		}

		FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.639 } }
	}
	after = {
		clean_up_after_duel_effect = yes
	}
}

# seduction.636 Duel: Accuser wins!
character_event = {
	id = seduction.636
	picture = GFX_evt_family_feud_republic
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = brave } NOT = { trait = craven } }
			random = {
				chance = 20
				add_trait = brave
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38270 days = 3 } # Brave gained
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { trait = craven }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				remove_trait = craven
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38296 days = 3 } # Craven lost
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { combat_rating_diff = { character = FROMFROM value = -30 } } }
			random_list = {
				70 = { set_character_flag = enemy_yields }
				15 = { set_character_flag = enemy_wounded FROMFROM = { add_trait = wounded } }
				10 = { set_character_flag = enemy_maimed FROMFROM = { maim_hand_effect = yes } }
				5 = { set_character_flag = enemy_killed }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 75
				random_list = {
					50 = { add_trait = wounded }
					50 = { maim_hand_effect = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { combat_rating_diff = { character = FROMFROM value = 0 } } }
			random_list = {
				50 = { set_character_flag = enemy_yields }
				25 = { set_character_flag = enemy_wounded FROMFROM = { add_trait = wounded } }
				15 = { set_character_flag = enemy_maimed FROMFROM = { maim_hand_effect = yes } }
				10 = { set_character_flag = enemy_killed }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 50
				random_list = {
					75 = { add_trait = wounded }
					25 = { maim_hand_effect = yes }
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { combat_rating_diff = { character = FROMFROM value = 30 } } }
			random_list = {
				25 = { set_character_flag = enemy_yields }
				40 = { set_character_flag = enemy_wounded FROMFROM = { add_trait = wounded } }
				20 = { set_character_flag = enemy_maimed FROMFROM = { maim_hand_effect = yes } }
				15 = { set_character_flag = enemy_killed }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 25
				random_list = {
					90 = { add_trait = wounded }
					10 = { maim_hand_effect = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {
				10 = { set_character_flag = enemy_yields }
				45 = { set_character_flag = enemy_wounded FROMFROM = { add_trait = wounded } }
				25 = { set_character_flag = enemy_maimed FROMFROM = { maim_hand_effect = yes } }
				20 = { set_character_flag = enemy_killed }
			}

			random = { chance = 10 add_trait = wounded }
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction636
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = wounded } NOT = { is_maimed_trigger = yes } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction636_wound
		trigger = { trait = wounded NOT = { is_maimed_trigger = yes } }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCseduction636_maim trigger = { is_maimed_trigger = yes } }

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction636
		trigger = { has_character_flag = enemy_yields }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROMFROMFROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.637 } }
			clr_character_flag = enemy_yields
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction636
		trigger = { has_character_flag = enemy_wounded }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROMFROMFROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.637 } }
			clr_character_flag = enemy_wounded
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseduction636
		trigger = { has_character_flag = enemy_maimed }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROMFROMFROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.637 } }
			clr_character_flag = enemy_maimed
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDseduction636
		trigger = { has_character_flag = enemy_killed }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROMFROMFROMFROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.637 } }
			clr_character_flag = enemy_killed
		}
	}
}

	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	show_from_from = yes

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction638
		trigger = { FROMFROM = { has_character_flag = enemy_yields } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction638_wound
		trigger = { FROMFROM = { has_character_flag = enemy_wounded } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction638_maim
		trigger = { FROMFROM = { has_character_flag = enemy_maimed } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseduction638_death
		trigger = { FROMFROM = { has_character_flag = enemy_killed } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction638
		trigger = { NOT = { FROMFROM = { has_character_flag = enemy_killed } } }
		remove_lover = FROM
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction638
		trigger = { NOT = { FROMFROM = { has_character_flag = enemy_killed } } }

		add_rival = FROMFROM

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = cruel } NOT = { trait = kind } }
			random = {
				chance = 25
				add_trait = cruel
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38259 } # Cruel gained
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { trait = kind }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				remove_trait = kind
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38307 } # Kind lost
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseduction638
		trigger = { FROMFROM = { has_character_flag = enemy_killed } }
		if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
		else = { death = { death_reason = death_duel killer = FROMFROM } }
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = seduction.700
	is_triggered_only = yes # seduction.650, the lovers' pulse (was MTTH 120 months)
	hide_window = yes

	capable_only = yes
	min_age = 16

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Way of Life"
		has_lover = yes
		NOT = { culture_group = daemonic_group }
		any_lover = {
			NOT = { culture_group = daemonic_group }
			NOT = { reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 25 } }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = gbc_courtly_lover }
		}
		NOT = { has_character_flag = gbc_courtly_lover }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_lover = {
			limit = { NOT = { reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 25 } } }
			character_event = { id = seduction.701 }
		}
	}
}

		clr_character_flag = lost_loving_feeling # Set in the Grand Judge chain (judge.42, judge.43 in WHG_court_office_events). Zina.
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction702 # Such is life. Married.
		trigger = { any_spouse = { character = FROM } }

		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }

		FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.703 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPlover52 } }
		clr_character_flag = lost_loving_feeling # Set in the Grand Judge chain (judge.42, judge.43 in WHG_court_office_events). Zina.
	}

			OR = {

		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_rekindle_love months = 24 }
	}

# seduction.704 Lover attempts re-seduction
character_event = {
	id = seduction.704
	desc = EVTDESCseduction704

	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction704 # Oh yes!
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 4.0 FROM = { has_character_modifier = wol_seducer_2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 5.0 FROM = { OR = { trait = seducer trait = seductress } } }
			modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = lustful }
		}

		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_rekindle_love months = 36 }

		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = seduction.705 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction705 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction704 # Nope
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = arbitrary }
			modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = chaste }
		}

		remove_lover = FROM
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.706 days = 3 } } }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The stress of many lovers
###############################################################################

character_event = {
	id = seduction.750
	is_triggered_only = yes # seduction.650, the lovers' pulse (was MTTH 80 months)
	desc = EVTDESCseduction750

	picture = GFX_evt_bad_news
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	hide_from = yes

	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Way of Life"
		NOT = { trait = stressed }
		num_of_lovers = 2

		any_lover = {
			NOT = { culture_group = daemonic_group }
			prisoner = no
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_lover = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { culture_group = daemonic_group }
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
			}
			save_event_target_as = first_lover
		}
		random_lover = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				event_target:first_lover = { NOT = { character = PREV } }
			}
			save_event_target_as = second_lover
		}
		random_lover = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				event_target:first_lover = { NOT = { character = PREV } }
				event_target:second_lover = { NOT = { character = PREV } }
			}
			save_event_target_as = third_lover
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction750 # Become stressed
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		add_trait = stressed
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction750 # End an affair
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		event_target:first_lover = { remove_lover = ROOT }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { event_target:second_lover = { always = yes } }
		name = EVTOPTCseduction750 # End an affair
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		event_target:second_lover = { remove_lover = ROOT }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDseduction750 # End an affair
		trigger = { event_target:third_lover = { always = yes } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		event_target:third_lover = { remove_lover = ROOT }
	}
}

# seduction.760 Starts with the lover
character_event = {
	id = seduction.760
	is_triggered_only = yes # seduction.650, the lovers' pulse (was MTTH 144 months)
	desc = EVTDESCseduction760

	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes
	has_dlc = "Way of Life"

	trigger = {
		any_lover = {
			is_married = yes
			has_polygamy = no
			in_command = no
			has_regent = no
			is_ill = no
			prisoner = no
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			spouse = {
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_lover = PREV }
				NOT = { opinion = { who = PREV value = 0 } }
			}
			reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 25 }
			NOT = {
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_seduced_yearning }
			}
		}
		is_ill = no
		in_command = no
		has_regent = no
	}

	immediate = {
		random_lover = {
			limit = {
				is_married = yes
				has_polygamy = no
				in_command = no
				has_regent = no
				is_ill = no
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				spouse = {
					prisoner = no
					NOT = { trait = incapable }
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_lover = PREV }
					NOT = { opinion = { who = PREV value = 0 } }
				}
				reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 25 }
				NOT = {
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = opinion_seduced_yearning
					}
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = my_lover
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction760 # Suggest time together
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		event_target:my_lover = {
			character_event = { id = seduction.761 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction761 }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_seduced_yearning months = 36 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction760 # Should not interfere
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# seduction.761 The married lover
character_event = {
	id = seduction.761
	desc = EVTDESCseduction761

	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction761 # Agree, country retreat
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = slothful }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = honest }
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = humble }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = stressed }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				remove_trait = stressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38313 days = 1 } # Notify lost Stressed
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = depressed }
			random = {
				chance = 25
				remove_trait = depressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38314 days = 1 } # Notify lost Depressed
				}
			}
		}

		add_character_modifier = { modifier = comfort_of_lover days = 365 }

		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = seduction.763 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction763 }
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random = { chance = 50 venereal_disease_effect = yes }
			if = {
				limit = {
					fertility = 0.33
					FROM = { fertility = 0.33 }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							is_woman_trigger = yes
							NOT = { age = 45 }
							is_pregnant = no
							FROM = { is_female = no }
						}
						AND = {
							is_female = no
							FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes NOT = { age = 45 } is_pregnant = no }
						}
					}
				}
				random = {
					chance = 25
					if = {
						limit = { FROM = { OR = { is_married = yes is_consort = yes } } }
						impregnate_cuckoo = FROM
					}
					else = { impregnate = FROM }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseduction761 # Do it right here

		trigger = { OR = { trait = depressed trait = stressed } }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 is_woman_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = arbitrary }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = deceitful }
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = stressed } remove_trait = stressed }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = depressed }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				remove_trait = depressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38314 days = 1 } # Notify lost Depressed
				}
			}
		}

		add_character_modifier = { modifier = thrill_of_lover days = 365 }

		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = seduction.762 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPseduction762 }
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random = { chance = 50 venereal_disease_effect = yes }
			if = {
				limit = {
					fertility = 0.33
					FROM = { fertility = 0.33 }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							is_woman_trigger = yes
							NOT = { age = 45 }
							is_pregnant = no
							FROM = { is_female = no }
						}
						AND = {
							is_female = no
							FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes NOT = { age = 45 } is_pregnant = no }
						}
					}
				}
				random = {
					chance = 25
					if = {
						limit = { FROM = { OR = { is_married = yes is_consort = yes } } }
						impregnate_cuckoo = FROM
					}
					else = { impregnate = FROM }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseduction761 # I don't need comfort
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = proud }
		}

		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_declined_romance months = 24 }

		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.764 days = 3 } } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDseduction761 # Lunatic text
		trigger = { OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
		tooltip_info_custom = lunatic
		ai_chance = { factor = 1000 }

		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_declined_romance months = 24 }

		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.764 days = 3 } } }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Monstrous lovers (was BR_WoL_seduction_events.txt, BRseduction.0 - 11)
###############################################################################

# seduction.800 The monstrous lovers' pulse (on_yearly_pulse)
# A player who takes a vampire or a servant of Chaos for a lover should
# fear the consequences. Once a year, an AI vampire or follower of Nurgle,
# Slaanesh, Khorne or Tzeentch with a mortal player lover may decide to
# show that lover what they truly are.
# seduction.800 Scope: the AI lover; event_target:monstrous_lover_victim is the player.
character_event = {
	id = seduction.800
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse

	ai = yes
	min_age = 16

	trigger = {
		any_lover = { ai = no }
		OR = {
			AND = { is_vampire_trigger = yes any_lover = { ai = no is_vampire_trigger = no } }
			AND = {
				true_religion_group = chaos_gods_group
				any_lover = { ai = no NOT = { true_religion_group = chaos_gods_group } }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random = {
			chance = 50
			if = {
				limit = { is_vampire_trigger = yes }
				random_lover = {
					limit = { ai = no is_vampire_trigger = no }
					save_event_target_as = monstrous_lover_victim
				}
				character_event = { id = seduction.801 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					true_religion = slaanesh
					any_lover = {
						ai = no
						NOT = { true_religion_group = chaos_gods_group }
						is_incorruptible_trigger = no
					}
				}
				random_lover = {
					limit = {
						ai = no
						NOT = { true_religion_group = chaos_gods_group }
						is_incorruptible_trigger = no
					}
					save_event_target_as = monstrous_lover_victim
				}
				character_event = { id = seduction.807 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = { true_religion = nurgle true_religion = khorne true_religion = tzeentch }
				}
				random_lover = {
					limit = { ai = no NOT = { true_religion_group = chaos_gods_group } }
					save_event_target_as = monstrous_lover_victim
				}
				if = { limit = { true_religion = nurgle } character_event = { id = seduction.805 } }
				else_if = {
					limit = { true_religion = khorne }
					character_event = { id = seduction.809 }
				}
				else = { character_event = { id = seduction.811 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# seduction.802 Mauled and drained: death, or scars and a lost limb
# seduction.802 Scope: the lover; FROM is the vampire.
character_event = {
	id = seduction.802
	desc = EVTDESCseduction802
	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes # seduction.801

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseduction802
		remove_lover = FROM
		random_list = {
			20 = { # Drained
				if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
				else = { death = { death_reason = death_murder killer = FROM } }
			}
			40 = { # Mauled
				add_trait = disfigured
				add_trait = maimed
				health = -2
				FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.804 } }
			}
			40 = { # Mauled and gelded
				modifier = { factor = 0 is_female = yes }
				add_trait = disfigured
				add_trait = maimed
				add_trait = eunuch
				health = -2
				FROM = { character_event = { id = seduction.804 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# seduction.808 A night of pain and pleasure: an elf turns to the cult of
# pleasure of their own people, anyone else to Slaanesh
# seduction.808 Scope: the lover; FROM is the follower of Slaanesh.
character_event = {
	id = seduction.808
	desc = EVTDESCseduction808
	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes # seduction.807

	option = { # We are done
		name = EVTOPTAseduction808
		remove_lover = FROM
		add_trait = wounded
		health = -1
	}

	option = { # I enjoyed it
		name = EVTOPTBseduction808
		add_trait = wounded
		health = -1
		if = { limit = { religion_group = cytharai_group } religion = atharti }
		else_if = { limit = { religion_group = cadai_group } religion = cult_of_pleasure }
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROM = { faith_fits_race_of_prev_trigger = yes } }
			religion = slaanesh
		}
	}
}

# seduction.809 The Khornate's test
# seduction.809 Scope: the follower of Khorne.
character_event = {
	id = seduction.809
	desc = EVTDESCseduction809
	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes # seduction.800

	option = { # Test them
		name = EVTOPTAseduction809
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 6 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 4 trait = lustful }
			modifier = { factor = 4 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 4 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = arbitrary }
		}
		event_target:monstrous_lover_victim = { character_event = { id = seduction.810 } }
	}

	option = { # Restraint
		name = EVTOPTseduction_restraint
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 6 trait = temperate }
			modifier = { factor = 4 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 4 trait = chaste }
			modifier = { factor = 4 trait = just }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }
		}
	}
}

# seduction.810 A Chaos Knight in the woods: flee, or fight for the Blood God
# seduction.810 Scope: the lover; FROM is the follower of Khorne.
character_event = {
	id = seduction.810
	desc = EVTDESCseduction810
	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes # seduction.809

	option = { # Flee
		name = EVTOPTAseduction810
		remove_lover = FROM
		add_trait = craven
		random = {
			chance = 20
			if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
			else = { death = { death_reason = death_battle } }
		}
	}

	option = { # Fight
		name = EVTOPTBseduction810
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = duel } }
		if = { limit = { FROM = { faith_fits_race_of_prev_trigger = yes } } religion = khorne }
		save_event_target_as = combatant_1
		set_character_flag = duel
		create_random_soldier = {
			name = RANDOM
			culture = aghol
			random_traits = yes
			dynasty = none
			religion = khorne
			female = no
			age = 30
		}
		new_character = {
			change_martial = 5
			add_trait = brave
			add_trait = chaos_knight
			save_event_target_as = combatant_2
			character_event = { id = duel.1 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# seduction.812 The change: a mutation, and perhaps Tzeentch's faith
# seduction.812 Scope: the lover; FROM is the follower of Tzeentch.
character_event = {
	id = seduction.812
	desc = EVTDESCseduction812
	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes # seduction.811

	option = { # We are done
		name = EVTOPTAseduction812
		remove_lover = FROM
		mutations_table = yes
	}

	option = { # Embrace the change
		name = EVTOPTBseduction812
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { faith_fits_race_of_prev_trigger = yes } }
			religion = tzeentch
			mutations_table_tzeentch = yes
		}
		else = { mutations_table = yes }
	}
}
